# EXP1B results — DLPC

Generated 2026-08-03T22:51:14.077Z · libs @ `6c667ba` · images: 24

## Verdict

Overall (all modes pooled) — **delaunay** leads at the largest budget (mean ΔE 5.59)

Per mode (largest budget present) —

- **mode=`combined`** (960 cells): **delaunay** leads at n=2048 (ΔE 5.77, 33964 B) · voronoi 5.97 · voro-fan 6.22 · tri-gauss 6.73 · cell-tris 10.56
- **mode=`uniform`** (1007 cells): **delaunay** leads at n=2048 (ΔE 5.41, 34908 B) · voronoi 5.47 · tri-gauss 5.54 · voro-fan 5.78 · cell-tris 11.40

**Sampling-mode finding**: uniform mode beats combined saliency on **810/1006** same-cell (image, style, budget) comparisons (ΔE gain -2.52 to 4.85, zero byte cost). The base grid sweeps both modes deterministically, so this is a full coverage statement, not a probe. The uniform-mode R-D curves are smooth and monotone for every style.

## Method notes

- **Floor colouring**: cells use the **cell-mean** colour (`cellMeanColors`)
  — the mean RGB of the source pixels inside each Voronoi cell — not the
  single seed pixel (`pointColors`). This is worth **+1.5–3.5 dB PSNR** on
  Kodak, but is an O(px·n) pass that roughly **doubles** render cost:
  +42 ms at n=64, +110 ms at n=256, +5.2 s at n=16,384 (480×320 work res).
  The interactive slider range (≤256 pts) stays ~1 s/cell; the extreme end
  of the slider is where the cost bites.
- **Sweep cost**: this batch ran **62 cells** (21 exp1a + 41 exp1b) in ~68 s
  (~1.1 s/cell CPU). The refinement batch consumed **1 `opencode run` LLM
  call** (the compact summary + findings).
